### Focused Verification Commands

The implementation stages add these exact public verification scopes, and each scope must reject
missing implementation rather than becoming a no-op. `AdminManagement` runs the following filters
separately and records a positive-count TRX for every family activated by the named task:

| Activation task | Exact filter |
| --- | --- |
| M35-03-T01 | `FullyQualifiedName~AdminAccessManagementAdministratorDomainTests` |
| M35-03-T01 | `FullyQualifiedName~AdminAccessManagementRoleDomainTests` |
| M35-03-T02 | `FullyQualifiedName~AdminAccessManagementAuthorizationTests` |
| M35-03-T02 | `FullyQualifiedName~AdminAccessManagementConcurrencyTests` |
| M35-04-T01 | `FullyQualifiedName~AdminAccessManagementSessionTests` |
| M35-04-T01 | `FullyQualifiedName~AdminAccessManagementMigrationTests` |
| M35-04-T01 | `FullyQualifiedName~AdminAccessManagementQueryTests` |
| M35-04-T02 | `FullyQualifiedName~AdminAccessManagementSeedTests` |
| M35-04-T03 | `FullyQualifiedName~AdminAccessManagementApiTests` |
| M35-04-T03 | `FullyQualifiedName~AdminAccessManagementOpenApiTests` |
| M35-06-T02 | `FullyQualifiedName~AdminAccessManagementComposedApiAcceptanceTests` |
| M35-03-T01 | retained `FullyQualifiedName~AdministratorAccessApiTests` regression |

```powershell
pwsh -NoProfile -File scripts/verify.ps1 -Scope AdminManagement
pwsh -NoProfile -File scripts/update-admin-access-contract.ps1 -Check
pwsh -NoProfile -File scripts/verify.ps1 -Scope AdminManagementAdmin
pwsh -NoProfile -File scripts/verify.ps1 -Scope AdminE2E
```

`AdminManagement` owns the neutral positive-count TRX helper and exact stage-aware manifest above.
It must neither invoke nor require a pending family's source, and it must not omit a family once
that family's activation task becomes `in_progress`, `blocked`, or `complete`. In particular, the
composed-acceptance filter is absent before M35-06-T02 and becomes mandatory and zero-test-failing
from M35-06-T02 onward.
`AdminManagementAdmin` owns the positive-count Vitest JSON helper and matches only
`admin/apps/web-ele/src/**/admin-access*.test.ts` and
`admin/apps/web-ele/src/views/admin-access/**/*.test.ts`; it also runs the app type check and
production build. `AdminE2E` runs exactly the four management tests in
`admin/apps/web-ele/e2e/admin-access.spec.ts` and the single retained GameConfig test. Server and
Admin cumulative scopes must be run sequentially because they share restored/build output.
Until M35-07-T01 is active and all four management tests plus the machine-count guard exist, direct
`-Scope AdminE2E` execution must fail as unavailable. The cumulative `Admin` baseline may continue
running the one pre-M3.5 GameConfig regression through M35-06; that run is regression evidence only.

### Real Acceptance And Cleanup

Every real run owns one lowercase `novagame-test-[a-f0-9]{12}` run ID. Docker project, database,
ports, API/Vite/Playwright/browser process tree, temporary environment, TRX/JSON, screenshots,
traces, reports, and logs are attributable to that ID. The API, Vite, Playwright and browser tree
executes in one Windows Job Object with kill-on-close semantics, so descendants remain owned even
after an intermediate launcher exits. A `finally` path runs on success and failure, snapshots
owned PID plus creation time, closes the Job Object to stop all owned processes, and executes
`docker compose down --volumes --remove-orphans` for the exact validated project. Cleanup failure
fails the command while retaining the original operation error.

Each launcher is created suspended and assigned before it is resumed. Assignment failure
terminates the suspended process through its original native handle and verifies its exit before
releasing that handle. A Job Object handle is cleared only after `CloseHandle` reports success;
fallback closure must not silently discard a live handle. If the primary close fails, the verifier
terminates the still-owned Job Object, verifies that it reports zero owned processes, and only then
retries `CloseHandle`; an injected-failure probe proves this path terminates a live child. Job
configuration failure uses the same verified close path, and an injected configuration-failure
probe must complete that close before returning the original failure.

The final cleanup assertion reports zero matching containers, networks, volumes, listening ports,
owned API/Vite/Playwright/browser process identities, and temporary secret/environment files.
All reporter output, test output and explicit screenshots resolve beneath
`.local/admin-management/<run-id>`. Successful evidence is removed only from that validated
absolute path; failed evidence may remain only there after all binary or compressed artifacts are
removed, raw child-process transcripts are deleted, and every retainable text artifact passes the
secret scan. Recursive removal outside that root is refused. User-owned `lefthook.yml` is never
scanned as milestone evidence, changed, staged, or removed.

On a successful browser result, the verifier validates the exact redacted response-audit reports.
The `finally` path then stops the complete Job Object process tree, drains the authenticated
dynamic-secret registry, removes Docker and temporary secrets, verifies ports and legacy outputs,
and only then decodes the exact allowlisted screenshots for IHDR bounds, dimensions, opacity,
pixel diversity, and SHA-256 identity. Immediately before retained-text scanning it rechecks each
validated PNG identity, scans exact configured and dynamic secrets plus UTF-8 and decoded-token
hash representations, and never reads child stdout/stderr into a thrown diagnostic. Only after
every check passes may it delete successful evidence. Any operation or cleanup failure uses an
empty screenshot allowlist and removes all binaries; an incomplete dynamic registry or failed
Playwright operation removes the complete run root rather than retaining possibly unscannable
text. Stopping the registry cancels only the wait for another connection: an already accepted
frame must finish or produce a protocol fault. Even after a fault, the verifier obtains a
cleanup-only snapshot of every value already accepted before scanning or deleting evidence, but
only after the collector worker has joined. A stop timeout cannot snapshot, dispose, or clear
worker-owned state. If join or snapshot completeness cannot be proved, failed evidence has no
retention fallback: only bounded, verified whole-run-root removal retries are allowed. A failed
secret scan enforces the same removal rule regardless of browser result or registry-completion
state. Real transient-lock, permanent-lock, and boundary probes verify the bounded deletion policy.

### Ordered Cumulative Verification

Run cumulative scopes sequentially, never concurrently. `AdminManagement` and checked contract
generation join this list after M35-04; `AdminManagementAdmin` joins after M35-05; `AdminE2E`
qualifies for M3.5 only after the four real management scenarios exist.

```powershell
pwsh -NoProfile -File scripts/verify.ps1 -Scope Server
pwsh -NoProfile -File scripts/verify.ps1 -Scope Database
pwsh -NoProfile -File scripts/verify.ps1 -Scope Authentication
pwsh -NoProfile -File scripts/verify.ps1 -Scope AdminManagement
pwsh -NoProfile -File scripts/update-admin-access-contract.ps1 -Check
pwsh -NoProfile -File scripts/verify.ps1 -Scope AdminManagementAdmin
pwsh -NoProfile -File scripts/verify.ps1 -Scope Admin
pwsh -NoProfile -File scripts/verify.ps1 -Scope Documentation
pwsh -NoProfile -File scripts/verify.ps1 -Scope Repository
git diff --check
```

The cumulative `Admin` scope already invokes the exact five-test `AdminE2E` path, so the ordered
set does not invoke `AdminE2E` a second time. M35-07 may run `AdminE2E` independently while browser
evidence is being established.

## M35-06 Real Server Acceptance Evidence Record

**Status:** Frozen and verified by M35-06-T01 on 2026-08-16. This record supplements, and must not alter,
the M35-02 Frozen Verification Contract. It defines the qualifying composed-server evidence for
M35-06-T02 and reserves adversarial security evidence for M35-06-T03.

### Evidence Rules

- Existing focused Domain, Application, persistence, API, and `WebApplicationFactory` tests remain
  supporting evidence only. They do not close the missing real composed-server boundary.
- M35-06-T02 qualifies only when its acceptance fixture starts the real out-of-process
  `NovaGame.Api` Kestrel host with production registrations, a disposable MySQL 8.4 instance, and
  `NovaGame.DbMigrator`, then drives post-bootstrap state through loopback HTTP.
- Only the configured bootstrap administrator may be seeded. Custom roles, administrators,
  permission assignments, sessions, and audit-producing state must be created through the frozen
  HTTP APIs after bootstrap.
- T02 must not use `WebApplicationFactory`, a mock backend, response interception, synthesized
  permissions, repository replacement, a second DbContext, or direct MySQL management-state
  mutation. Browser or Vben evidence is not T02 evidence.
- Raw MySQL is read-only for durable-state assertions after the relevant HTTP command. It must not
  arrange, mutate, delete, or repair management state. Test-only fault injection is reserved for
  T03 and must not create management state or replace the composed production path.
- `pwsh -NoProfile -File scripts/verify.ps1 -Scope AdminManagement` is the qualifying command for
  every matrix row. A direct test invocation is diagnostic only, and a zero-test filter fails.
- T02 records failed commands and cleanup failures in this Goal. A failed prerequisite, skipped
  test, leaked disposable resource, or substituted dependency never qualifies as passing evidence.

### Exact M35-06 Acceptance Commands

M35-06-T01 is complete only after this documentation-governance sequence passes in order:

```powershell
pwsh -NoProfile -File scripts/verify.ps1 -Scope Documentation
pwsh -NoProfile -File scripts/verify.ps1 -Scope Repository
pwsh -NoProfile -Command "[scriptblock]::Create((Get-Content -Raw scripts/verify.ps1)) | Out-Null"
git diff --check
```

M35-06-T02 adds this qualifying composed-server command:

```powershell
pwsh -NoProfile -File scripts/verify.ps1 -Scope AdminManagement
```

The enabled `AdminManagement` manifest must execute the positive-count
`AdminAccessManagementComposedApiAcceptanceTests` family once T02 is active. Its acceptance
fixture owns the Kestrel, DbMigrator, disposable-MySQL, loopback-HTTP, result-parsing, and cleanup
lifecycle. The existing focused tests and a direct `dotnet test` invocation remain supporting or
diagnostic evidence only.

M35-06-T03 extends the same qualifying command only after T02 is complete. It must not redefine
the six T02 rows or use a browser suite as server evidence. M35-06-T04 reruns the qualifying
command and then the Documentation, Repository, PowerShell parse, and whitespace checks above.

### Frozen T02 Boundary

The future acceptance artifact is exactly
`server/tests/NovaGame.EntityFrameworkCore.Tests/AdminAccessManagementComposedApiAcceptanceTests.cs`.
It remains absent until M35-06-T02 is activated. The pre-registered stage-aware filter is not
authorization to create it early.

T02 uses the real out-of-process `NovaGame.Api` Kestrel host and production registrations with
disposable MySQL 8.4 and `NovaGame.DbMigrator`. It must not use `WebApplicationFactory`, a mock
backend, response interception, synthesized permissions, repository replacement, a second
DbContext, direct MySQL management-state mutation, or browser/Vben evidence. Only the configured
bootstrap administrator may be seeded; every later management object and session must be created
through frozen HTTP commands.

Read-only raw-MySQL durable-state assertions are allowed only after the HTTP command being proved.
They may not establish test inputs, mutate rows, repair data, or generate acceptance state.
Test-only fault injection is reserved for T03 and may only cause a real composed operation to fail;
it must not synthesize an API result, replace a production dependency, or create management state.

The following are deliberately not T02 claims and remain reserved for T03: denial order and
isolated leaf-permission failures; self-lockout and built-in-role protection; stale revisions and
canonical no-ops; replay, fingerprint conflict, and concurrent commands; secret/log redaction
probes; and transactional audit-failure rollback.

## M35-01 - Activate And Audit The Baseline

### Actions

- Record the explicit authority, exact baseline commit, branch, scope, non-goals, stages, and tasks.
- Register M3.5 as the only active roadmap goal without renaming or activating M4.
- Extend repository verification to recognize the goal, stage sequence, and current task.
- Audit existing AdminAccess behavior and generic-admin surfaces before proposing implementation details.

### Verification

```powershell
pwsh -NoProfile -File scripts/verify.ps1 -Scope Documentation
pwsh -NoProfile -File scripts/verify.ps1 -Scope Repository
git diff --check
```

### Complete When

The current-state audit and gap matrix are evidence-backed, M4 remains `Unscheduled`, no
implementation source changed before design, all commands pass, and only M35-02 becomes active.

## M35-02 - Freeze Management Contracts

### Actions

- Freeze domain transitions and invariants for administrator, role, permission, and session state.
- Freeze authorization, concurrency, idempotency, transaction, audit, error, and query behavior.
- Freeze persistence mappings, migration changes, permission seed behavior, API contracts, admin
  interactions, and generated-contract workflow.
- Freeze the exact requirement-to-evidence matrix and stage-aware repository guards.

### Verification

Run documentation and repository guards, C# formatting and baseline builds, admin formatting and
type checks, plus executable reviews that reject unresolved design values and premature source.

### Complete When

Every approved behavior has one owner, one contract, explicit failures, and named evidence; no
open design choice can materially change implementation; and only M35-03 becomes active.

## M35-03 - Implement Domain And Application

### Actions

- Implement only the approved aggregate transitions and value validation in Domain.
- Add explicit request/response contracts and application orchestration.
- Enforce permissions before sensitive lookup, then concurrency and transactional mutation.
- Coordinate password hashing, session revocation, idempotency, and audit through injected ports.
- Add focused Domain and Application tests for happy paths and every frozen failure.

### Verification

Run exact focused Domain and Application tests, C# formatting, solution builds, Documentation, and
Repository scopes named in M35-02.

### Complete When

Domain and Application behavior passes without HTTP, EF Core, or UI policy leaking into the wrong
layer, and only M35-04 becomes active.

## M35-04 - Complete Persistence And API Integration

### Actions

- Implement bounded repository queries, locks, mappings, indexes, and the explicit MySQL migration.
- Register management permissions and synchronize the built-in role idempotently.
- Add thin controllers with accurate status, error, ETag, paging, and OpenAPI contracts.
- Add real MySQL and API tests for state, authorization, audit atomicity, concurrency, and replay.
- Generate TypeScript contracts through the frozen checked workflow and protect existing
  contracts from drift.

### Verification

Run migration listing and pending-model checks, focused API/OpenAPI tests, real MySQL integration
filters, contract regeneration, server verification, Documentation, Repository, and whitespace.

### Complete When

The migration applies to MySQL 8.4, twice-run seed is stable, APIs match the frozen contract,
contracts regenerate identically, integrations pass, and only M35-05 becomes active.

## M35-05 - Implement The Vben Management Workspace

### Actions

- Add typed transport and permission-controlled routes for the four management workspaces.
- Implement compact tables, filters, drawers, dialogs, confirmations, and exact retry behavior.
- Keep server state authoritative and prevent stale responses from replacing newer view state.
- Cover loading, empty, validation, unauthorized, forbidden, conflict, error, and retry states.
- Add focused unit and component tests without using a mock backend as integration evidence.

### Verification

Run the pinned admin formatter, linters, type checks, focused unit/component tests, production
build, contract guard, generic-boundary guard, Documentation, Repository, and whitespace.

### Complete When

All four workspaces behave correctly under permission and failure states, remain usable at the
supported widths, and only M35-06 becomes active.

## M35-06 - Verify The Real Server Workflow

### Actions

- Run the frozen management lifecycle through the composed NovaGame API and disposable MySQL 8.4.
- Verify permission changes affect authorization, forced revocation blocks refresh, and audit
  evidence is committed atomically with authoritative mutations.
- Exercise stale revisions, idempotent replay, conflicting replay, concurrent commands,
  self-lockout guards, built-in-role protection, and transaction failure.
- Inspect logs for required structured context and prohibited secrets.

### Verification

Run the exact M35-06 real-API acceptance command, cumulative server/database verification, cleanup
audit, Documentation, Repository, and whitespace.

### Complete When

Every frozen server behavior has non-mock evidence, durable state matches, no secret is exposed,
all owned resources are removed, and only M35-07 becomes active.

## M35-07 - Verify The Real Browser Workflow

### Actions

- Run the complete operations workflow in Playwright against the composed API and real MySQL.
- Verify route and action filtering plus authoritative 401 and 403 responses.
- Exercise desktop and mobile layouts and all significant operational states.
- Capture and inspect acceptance screenshots, then remove only disposable output after evidence is recorded.

### Verification

Run the exact M35-07 Playwright command, admin format/lint/type/unit/build checks, real Admin scope,
cleanup audit, Documentation, Repository, and whitespace.

### Complete When

Desktop and mobile paths pass without mock responses, visual inspection has no unresolved layout
finding, cleanup is deterministic, and only M35-08 becomes active.

## M35-08 - Repository Acceptance And Closure

### Actions

- Run the exact ordered cumulative acceptance set frozen by M35-02 and M35-06.
- Audit dependency direction, ownership, permissions, contracts, migrations, secrets, logs,
  NovaCore, SwordMaster isolation, reproducibility, and disposable resources.
- Synchronize README, architecture, roadmap, goal, and scoped instructions where durable behavior changed.
- Remove only verified ignored M3.5-local evidence.
- Set `status: complete`, `active_stage: none`, and `execution_authorized: false` only after final verification.

### Verification

```powershell
pwsh -NoProfile -File scripts/verify.ps1
pwsh -NoProfile -File scripts/verify.ps1 -Scope Repository
git diff --check
```

Also run the frozen final migration, contract, NovaCore, secret, log, process, Docker, browser, and
local-evidence audits.

### Complete When

All tasks and exit criteria are complete, final verification passes, no owned resource remains,
M4 is still `Unscheduled`, documentation is current, and M3.5 is deactivated.

## M3.5 Exit Criteria

- Authorized operators can manage administrator accounts and custom roles through server-authoritative APIs.
- Registered permissions can be assigned to custom roles without weakening built-in-role or self-lockout guards.
- Session families can be inspected and forcibly revoked without exposing token material.
- Administrator-access audit history is filterable and every privileged mutation has atomic audit evidence.
- Stable permissions, errors, concurrency, idempotency, paging, and redaction rules are verified.
- The Vben `web-ele` workspaces pass focused tests and real desktop/mobile browser acceptance.
- MySQL 8.4 migrations, twice-run seed, pending-model checks, OpenAPI, and generated contracts pass.
- NovaGame contains no SwordMaster dependency or game-owned operations page.
- NovaCore remains unchanged, M4 remains `Unscheduled`, and all repository verification passes.

## Non-Goals

- SwordMaster or another game's administrators, players, equipment, grants, inventory, rewards, economy, or gameplay.
- Battle, Skills, Enhancement, Set Bonuses, Drops, Shop, Mail, Ranking, Activity, or game-owned live operations.
- Player-account support, player search, impersonation, bans, moderation, customer support, or analytics.
- Multi-factor authentication, external identity providers, SSO, SCIM, tenant management, or organization hierarchy.
- Password self-service, email delivery, approval workflows, bulk import, bulk mutation, or scheduled changes.
- Deleting administrators, roles, or audit history.
- A generic UI schema, dynamic route service, application-specific scripting language, or plugin marketplace.
- Extracting an admin shell, creating a physical module/package, or promoting an experimental contract.
- M4 portability work, a second client engine, a second game consumer, or NovaCore changes.
